## Counterexample traces

Each trace starts from an initial state. Step 1 shows the whole state; later steps show only what changed.

### TrackedStatus, ExpiryPolicy = "when_agree": DoneWins fails (invariant)

```
1. Init: child.t1 = "not_started", child.t2 = "not_started", child.t3 = "not_started", setStatus = "none", hasReason = false, override = "calculated_only", inbox = []
2. RuleUpdatesChild(t1, on_track): child.t1 = "on_track"
3. RuleUpdatesChild(t1, done): child.t1 = "done"
4. RuleUpdatesChild(t2, on_track): child.t2 = "on_track"
5. RuleUpdatesChild(t2, done): child.t2 = "done"
6. RuleUpdatesChild(t3, on_track): child.t3 = "on_track"
7. OwnerSets(at_risk): setStatus = "at_risk", override = "reason_required", inbox = ["status_set_with_no_reason"]

2. Feedback(f1): c.f1.st = "logged", c.f1.text = "real", seen = ["f1"], n = 1, who = ["kemar"], by = "kemar", ev = "LOGGED"
3. SetsHelp(kemar, false): allow.kemar = false, ev = "OPTED_OUT"
4. Accept(f1): c.f1.st = "in_eval_set", c.f1.text = null, evalSet = [{"id":"f1","words":"seed"}], by = "dev", ev = "ACCEPT"
```

```

```

### OwnerAsk, bug: accept ignores what the author saw: NeverOverwriteUnseen fails (action property)

```
1. Init: st = "none", record = "a", age = 0, appliedBy = null, told = false, inProject.kemar = true, inProject.jodian = true, inProject.alicia = true, inbox = []
2. Ask: st = "asked", inbox = ["owner_ask"]

3. Tick: today = 1, by = null, via = "timer"
4. Tick: today = 2
5. Tick: today = 3
```
